namespace aweXpect.Options
{
    public abstract class EnumerableQuantifier
    {
        protected EnumerableQuantifier() { }
        public abstract void AppendResult(System.Text.StringBuilder stringBuilder, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars grammars, int matchingCount, int notMatchingCount, int? totalCount, string? verb = null);
        public abstract aweXpect.Core.Constraints.Outcome GetOutcome(int matchingCount, int notMatchingCount, int? totalCount);
        public virtual aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier.QuantifierContexts GetQuantifierContext() { }
        public abstract bool IsDeterminable(int matchingCount, int notMatchingCount);
        public abstract bool IsSingle();
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier All(aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier AtLeast(int minimum, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier AtMost(int maximum, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier Between(int minimum, int maximum, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier Exactly(int expected, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier LessThan(int maximum, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier MoreThan(int minimum, aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        public static aweXpect.Options.EnumerableQuantifier None(aweXpect.Core.ExpectationGrammars expectationGrammars = 0) { }
        [System.Flags]
        public enum QuantifierContexts
        {
            MatchingItems = 2,
            NotMatchingItems = 4,
            None = 0,
        }
    }
    public class FixedCheckInterval : aweXpect.Options.ICheckInterval
    {
        public FixedCheckInterval(System.TimeSpan interval) { }
        public System.TimeSpan NextCheckInterval() { }
    }
    public interface ICheckInterval
    {
        System.TimeSpan NextCheckInterval();
    }
    public class RepeatedCheckOptions
    {
        public static readonly System.TimeSpan DefaultInterval;
        public RepeatedCheckOptions() { }
        public aweXpect.Options.ICheckInterval Interval { get; }
        public System.TimeSpan Timeout { get; }
        public void CheckEvery(System.TimeSpan interval) { }
        public override string ToString() { }
        public void Within(System.TimeSpan timeout) { }
    }
}
